# Fair Market Rents: Oklahoma

- Postal code: OK
- State FIPS: 40
- Counties: 77
- Towns: 0

HUD's Fair Market Rents for Oklahoma, fiscal years 1983 to 2027: 77 counties, each with its rents by bedroom count and the FMR areas it has sat in. 3 of them have changed FMR area at least once (not counting 2006, when HUD renumbered every area).
